### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 美國期中選舉由民主黨贏得參議院及眾議院多數席次，川普剩餘兩年任內將面
臨分立政府的局面，市場擔憂民主黨未來傾向加強AI監管，恐削弱美國經濟成
長動能。
 歐元區 10月 CPI年增率自前月的 3.8%上升至 4%，創下三年來的新高，通膨面
臨上行風險。歐洲央行管委暨斯洛伐克央行總裁卡日米爾(Peter Kazimir)表示，
歐洲央行將持續升息，以防止高通膨根深蒂固；德國央行總裁納格爾 (Joachim
Nagel)亦表示通膨前景尚未改善，歐洲央行應於 12 月升息。多位歐洲央行官員
發表偏鷹言論，市場預期歐洲央行未來一年內將再升息二至三次，升息預期升
溫，預計將支撐歐元走勢。
 美國 10 月 ISM 製造業 PMI 為 55.5，高於市場預期，製造業擴張速度加快；另
10 月 ISM非製造業 PMI為 54.5，持穩於景氣榮枯線之上，顯示美國整體基本面
佳，預計將為美元提供支撐。

 美國 10 月非農就業人數增加 16.5 萬人，優於市場預期的 6.5 萬人，另 10 月失
業率亦由前月的 4.2%降至 4.1%，顯示勞動市場仍具韌性，在美國經濟穩健及通
膨維持高位的情況下，強化聯準會 12月升息預期。
 澳洲央行於 11/3 將現金基準利率調升一碼至 4.85%，係今年以來第五次升息並
創下 2008 年 11 月以來新高水準。然而，此次九名委員中有三人主張維持利率
不變，投票結果出現分歧，加上貨幣政策委員會聲明中，刪除有關可能進一步升
息的明確表述，令市場預期澳洲央行將於 12月暫停升息。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.500，低點在 33.040，以 32.768作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 2 角 9 分。 
    上週台幣走貶，週初，台股開低，市場對AI投資支出及科技股估值的疑慮，致
使外資連續多日賣超台股並匯出，台幣連貶數日，加以週間美國期中選舉結果出爐，
民主黨勝選，市場擔憂 AI產業監管趨嚴，台股重挫，外資上週累計賣超台股 5,244
億，外資強勢匯出之下，儘管逢高有出口商拋匯抑制台幣貶值速度，台幣仍貶破33
整數大關，一度觸及年內低點 33.040，惟週五午盤過後，外資及壽險轉向匯入，加
以出口商拋匯需求，台幣跌幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.768。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。就國際市場而言，美國非農就業數據意外高於預期、
通膨壓力仍在，強化市場對於聯準會12月升息預期，高利率將有助國際美元走升；
就台灣市場來看，市場對AI產業資本支出、融資成本及信用風險產生疑慮，加以民
主黨勝選，引發市場對AI監管政策收緊與資料中心建設放緩的擔憂，外資持續賣超
台股，令台幣持續面臨貶值的壓力，惟出口商拋匯需求待消化，將抑制台幣貶值速
度，綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.600-33.200

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣先升後貶。週初，國際美元偏弱震盪，人民幣受益走升，境內人民
幣相隔四年多首次升破 6.65整數大關 ； 惟週二中國央行宣布調升外匯存款準備金率 ，
顯示其抑制單邊升值預期的意圖，人民幣大幅走貶；週間，市場持續消化人行政策
影響，人民幣走貶，最低一度貶至 6.6855，不過美國期中選舉結果出爐，市場不確
定性下降，國際油價、美債殖利率及美元指數皆下跌，緩解人民幣的賣壓，惟中國
國有大行逢低持續買進美元，人民幣反彈力道有限，終場境外人民幣收在 6.6736，
週線貶值 212 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6763，週線貶值 203 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣區間震盪。就國際市場來看，美國非農就業人數新增 16.5 萬
人，就業率下降至 4.1%，勞動力市場意外強勁，強化市場對於聯準會 12 月再次升
息的預期，利差因素支撐美元指數反彈，將抑制人民幣走升；就中國國內市場來看，
人行最新政策顯示其抑制人民幣單邊升值的意圖，惟第四季為結匯旺季，出口商實
質拋匯需求部分緩解人民幣貶值壓力；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，
USD/CNH重返五日均線之上，顯示人民幣強勢升值的趨勢暫歇，綜合以上，預估本
週人民幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6600-6.6900

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週日圓走弱。週初，市場等待美國期中選舉結果，交投偏謹慎觀望，USD/JPY
在 158 附近波動；週間，美國期中選舉由民主黨取得參眾議院多數席次，政治僵局
擔憂令美元走弱，USD/JPY最低來到 157.39；臨近週末，美國公布就業數據優於市
場預期，USD/JPY止跌回升，終場收在 158.45，週線上漲 100 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於季度展望報告中
表示，日本近期實際利率上升，基礎通膨率已加速升至2%附近，加上日本央行近期
亦強調政策已進入避免通膨超標為重點的新階段，進一步強化了其鷹派立場，市場
預期日本最快將於 12月再次升息，預計將支撐日圓走升。二、美國10月 ISM製造
業 PMI 為 55.5，優於市場預期，顯示製造業擴張速度加快；另外，美國公布 10 月
非農就業人數增加 16.5 萬人，優於市場預期的 6.5 萬人，美國經濟強勁、勞動市場
仍具韌性，強化聯準會12月升息預期，美元持穩，預計將有利USD/JPY走勢。三、
日本財務大臣片山皋月重申美日聯合匯率聲明仍具效力，將於必要時採取外匯干預
行動，以維護外匯市場有序運作，將限縮USD/JPY上行空間。四、就技術面而言，
在日線圖中，USD/JPY短中天期均線趨平，近期大致在157 至 158.50間盤整，匯價
暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：157.00-159.50 
前週交易區間 
157.21 158.76

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 先上後下。週初，在美伊傳出有望和談消息後，歐元自低點小
幅走升至 1.1237；週間，美國民主黨於期中選舉大獲全勝，川普選舉失利，美元偏
弱震盪；臨近週末，美國公布非農就業人數高於市場預期，歐元自高點回落，終場
收在 1.1143，週線下跌 12點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐元區10月 CPI年增率自
前月的 3.8%上升至 4%，創下三年來的新高，通膨面臨上行風險。此外，歐洲央行
管委暨斯洛伐克央行總裁卡日米爾(Peter Kazimir)表示，歐洲央行將持續升息，以防
止高通膨根深蒂固，並表示目前主要考量已非是否需採取進一步行動，而是須衡量
應升息幾次方能將通膨拉回 2%目標水準，市場預期歐洲央行未來一年內將再升息
二至三次，升息預期升溫，預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、美國公布10月非農就業人數
增加 16.5 萬人，優於市場預期的 6.5 萬人，另 10 月失業率亦由前月的 4.2%降至
4.1%，顯示勞動市場仍具韌性，強化了聯準會12月升息預期，美元再度走升至102，
預計將限縮歐元走升空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD各天期均線向
下發散，近期匯價多次向上測試1.1270均拉回留下長上影線，顯示上方仍有賣壓待
消化。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1030-1.1250 
前週交易區間 
1.1130 1.1272

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.11.02-115.11.06 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.825%  
30天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.69%  
公債－10 年期-A15109R 1.885% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.725%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，受台股重挫影響，令週五資金需求上揚，但提存期初市場
資金充裕，短率持穩於近期區間。 
週二央行標售 364天期定存單 1100億元，加權平均得標利率為1.577%，低於
市場預期區間 1.59%-1.61%，較上次標售結果下跌 2.5個基本點。 
週五隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.805%~0.825%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.67%~1.69%，利率較上週上揚 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投維持冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109R及 5年期指標券 A15108均
未成交，週五等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.885%、1.725%。 
股市承壓帶動避險需求，美債利率下滑，令先前台債偏空氛圍緩解，週三標售
30年期公債得標利率 2.267%， 低於市場預期區間2.27%~2.30%， 投標倍數1.85
倍，較上次 1.55 倍提升，不過考量台灣年底仍有升息可能，將抑制買盤向下
追價意願，預估台債利率區間盤整，預估 5 年券區間為 1.70%~1.75%，10 年
券區間為 1.85%~1.90%。

市場焦點
期中選舉
• 美國期中選舉後，市場擔憂民主黨可能放緩AI資料中心的建設並提高對AI的監管，促使科技股
面臨估值修正壓力 。但從基本面觀察 ，2026年標普500企業獲利年增率預估已由 7月的26.5%
大幅上修至36.1%，其中資訊科技產業預估成長 64.5%，通訊服務亦有52.0%。大型科技企業
仍具備穩健現金流與持續投入AI基礎建設的能力，AI長期發展趨勢尚未改變，產業基本面不宜
因短期估值修正而過度看淡。
中國A股
• 短期來看A股反彈行情有機會延續 ，主因為美國期中選舉落幕 、中國政策環境偏寬鬆 ，以及A
股估值與情緒指標落在歷史相對低檔 ，具備超跌反彈條件，政策面方面，人行可能進一步降息，
政策利率由1.25%下調至1%，搭配IPO超募資金回購、穩定資金加大ETF買入力道，有助於改

善市場流動性並提振投資信心。
石油/黃金
• 波斯灣國家能源出口替代方案已略見成效 ，不過由於替代路徑運輸成本較高 ，能源價格仍居
高不下，觀察過去幾週出口及價格變化 ，推測在美伊關係未有明確變化的情境下 ，國際油價
將在每桶100美元附近震盪。
• 央行購金需求維持高檔 ，但相較去年同期累計未進一步放大 。在此情境下 ，短中期黃金價格
能否進一步突破的關鍵，可能取決於聯準會貨幣政策的調整空間。

• 選民不滿情緒發酵，民主黨在期中選舉取得重大突破：伊朗局勢久拖不決、通膨持續攀升，削弱選民對川普政府的支持，民
主黨因此在期中選舉中於參、眾兩院取得過半席次，新一屆國會將在2027年1月3日就職，進入白宮與國會分由不同政黨掌
控的「分裂政府」時代。州長選戰方面，民主黨也成功翻轉多個原由共和黨長期執政的州，州長席次占比睽違20年再度超越
共和黨，其中德州、喬治亞州、俄亥俄州及愛荷華州的選情翻轉尤其受到矚目。
• 國會制衡與州政府版圖變化，政策走向仍存不確定性： 儘管民主黨成功奪回眾議院，但在參議院尚未取得足以單獨推動重大
法案的關鍵席次，例如除了財政預算相關法案可以用預算調節程序簡單多數通過外，一般的法案都需要60票才能通過，因此
未來重大政策仍須仰賴跨黨協商，市場對政策劇烈轉向的疑慮可望有所緩解。不過，民主黨掌握更多州政府，也可能促使部

分州在川普剩餘兩年任期內，進一步制衡或抵制聯邦施政議程，並對2028年總統大選的選務布局產生重要影響。

• 美國經濟具韌性，紮根美股未來底氣：美國期中選舉後，市場擔憂民主黨可能放
緩AI資料中心的建設並提高對AI的監管，促使科技股面臨估值修正壓力。但從基

鏈；然而，大型科技企業仍具備穩健現金流與持續投入AI基礎建設的能力，AI長
期發展趨勢尚未改變，產業基本面不宜因短期估值修正而過度看淡。

顧
美國政治藍色浪潮來襲：AI數據中心建設受阻與產業鏈資產重估
• 政治監管與地方風向轉變：AI基礎設施擴建面臨停工與審查的壓力，民主黨在多個關鍵州分贏得州長選舉，並伴隨地方公投
限制措施的升溫，使得原本蓬勃發展的AI數據中心建設計畫大幅放緩。
• 關鍵州分政策轉嚴：德州、喬治亞州與俄亥俄州等AI數據中心投資重鎮，當選民主黨州長普遍對數據中心開發採取強硬立場。
德州喊出暫停新建並取消稅收優惠，喬治亞州提出全州暫緩新項目，俄亥俄州與威斯康辛州亦對能源消耗、環境審查及稅收
補貼提出嚴格管制條件。
• 地方公投與審查週期拉長： 全美46項涉及數據中心的地方限制措施中 ，高達40項趨向收緊或已通過更嚴格限制 ，未來開發
項目將面臨更長審批週期、更嚴格電網接入審查及更高公用事業與監管合規成本。

• 未成熟開發商延期風險高：以全美儲備規模最大的德州為例，超過半數項目尚處於早期階段，且大量由缺乏營運經驗的初次
開發商主導。在監管與水電資源收緊下，此類項目極易面臨延期或取消的風險。
AI建設熱潮面臨民主黨藍色浪潮衝擊 46項地方數據中心措施投票結果分布
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.11.05

美國製造業景氣加速擴張，原物料成本壓力仍牽制通膨降溫
• 製造業擴張動能增強，生產與新訂單同步改善：美國10月ISM製造業PMI升至55.5，較9月54.5增加1%，連續第10個月
維持擴張；其中生產指數升至 59.0，創2021年11月以來新高，新訂單指數亦升至 55.5，顯示製造業接單及生產活動持
續增溫，且存貨指數回升至51.4，反映企業庫存由收縮轉為擴張，製造業景氣復甦動能進一步增強。
• 需求回升伴隨成本壓力，通膨降溫仍面臨挑戰：供應商交貨指數升至59.1，代表交貨速度持續放緩，連續11個月呈現相
同趨勢；客戶端庫存指數維持41.6，顯示庫存水位仍偏低，有利後續補庫存需求。然而，原物料價格指數雖由77.9降至
75.2，仍連續第25個月處於上漲區間，反映鋼鋁、石化及進口商品成本壓力尚未消退，可能使通膨降溫進程更加緩慢。

• 後市觀察：美國製造業景氣持續擴張，反映企業需求及經濟活動仍具韌性，有助支撐企業營收與獲利；惟原物料價格維
持高檔，可能增加通膨黏著性，使聯準會貨幣政策維持審慎，並對美債殖利率形成支撐。後續市場將關注製造業需求能
否延續，以及成本壓力是否逐步傳導至終端物價。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.11.3

企業財務與行業分化：高槓桿新雲業者受創，巨頭發揮定價優勢
• 政策與審查趨嚴使得整體AI建設成本上揚，但在產業內卻呈現出極端的強者恆強、弱者面臨淘汰的兩極趨勢。
• 新雲公司(Neoclouds)面臨流動性與違約風險：若數據中心建設延誤或無法如期接入電網，收入推遲產生，而這類新興雲端
服務商普遍具高財務槓桿，高額利息費用持續累積，導致現金流期限錯配，將提高其再融資風險、擴大信用利差，甚至觸發
合約中的不可抗力條款與債務違約風險。
• 超大規模雲端巨頭(Hyperscalers)享受供應趨緊紅利：對科技巨頭而言，基礎設施擴建受阻反而是利多。已擁有現成數據中
心與電力保障的巨頭定價權大幅提升，算力供給緊俏能將雲端計算服務價格維持在高檔。此外，巨頭有能力自行籌資進行專
用發電或直接與公用事業公司談判，進一步拉開與新進入者的差距。

• AI六大支柱亮警報：支撐AI熱潮持續發展六大條件(訂單積壓、融資、算力需求、競爭、政治、聯準會)中，政治、競爭及聯
準會三項已現疲軟，特別是詞元(Token)成本持續下降、模型可替代性提高，低價模型加劇競爭，持續擠壓軟體獲利空間。
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.11.05
詞元(T oken)成本持續下降• 小結與投資建議： 期中選舉引發的政策變革 ，正將AI
行業從無差別大舉擴建帶入嚴格監管與高成本的新階
段。供給側瓶頸(電網、土地、監管)將淘汰財務體質孱
弱、高度依賴外部融資的高槓桿新興開發商；供給受
限也鞏固已具備規模與資源保障的雲端巨頭壟斷優勢
與定價權。 投資建議避開高槓桿新雲與中小型開發商 ，
聚焦資本雄厚且自備水電資源的超大規模雲端巨頭 ，
密切關注聯準會貨幣政策與信用利差變化 ，AI行業的
金融條件正在收緊 ，若聯準會降息放慢或信用利差持
續擴大 ，AI供應鏈中的高估值軟體與硬體股可能面臨
新一輪估值修正。

AI資本支出持續攀升，現金流承壓加劇企業信用分化
• AI基礎建設投資擴大 ，企業融資需求同步升溫： AI競賽已由
模型及技術開發延伸至資料中心 、運算晶片與電力基礎設施
建置 ，帶動大型科技企業持續提高資本支出 。Microsoft、
Alphabet、Amazon等企業近期公布的財報均顯示 ，AI相關
投資已進入高強度建置階段 ，市場預估大型科技企業 2026年
AI基礎建設投資將超過7,000億美元，龐大資金需求也使市場
更加關注企業融資成本、資本配置效率及未來現金流表現。
• 財報反映投資成本，現金流與信用評等成市場檢驗重點： Q3
財報顯示，Alphabet及Amazon均因AI基礎建設投資大幅增
加，使自由現金流承受壓力；其中 Amazon近12個月自由現
金流轉為負值，Alphabet單季亦出現負自由現金流 。相較之

下，Microsoft仍維持正向自由現金流，反映企業財務承受能
力有所差異。此外，Meta因AI資本支出規模擴大及未來現金
流不確定性 ，遭標普全球調降信用評等展望至負向 ，顯示市
場已開始將AI投資效益納入企業信用風險評估。
• 市場看法： AI長期需求仍具成長潛力 ，但市場評價將逐步由
資本支出規模轉向投資報酬率 、自由現金流及財務槓桿管理 。
而具備穩定營運現金流 、較高資本回收效率及明確 AI商業化
成果的企業 ，較有能力維持投資動能；反之 ，若資本支出持
續攀升而營收及獲利未能同步跟上 ，將增加企業融資需求與
信用風險，並使科技企業間的評價差異進一步擴大。
大型科技企業擴大資本支出，承諾規模持續攀升
AI產業表外承諾大幅揚升
資料來源：Morgan Stanley Research，企業10-Q、10-K、8-K申報文件，ZeroHedge，
兆豐銀行彙整。

A股短期反彈行情可望延續，長期聚焦科技成長
• A股短期反彈行情可望延續 ，長期聚焦科技成長 ：從A股內部條件來看 ，
市場經歷長時間修正後 ，估值與情緒指標已降至歷史低檔 ，創業板 本
益比回落至約26.5%，全市場站上200日均線個股占比僅約一成 ，顯示
悲觀情緒已相當充分反映 ，搭配市場成交熱度回升 ，盤面觀察近期銀
行、消費等權值板塊 逐漸止穩，科技成長類股也開始出現籌碼與評價
修復，顯示市場輪動條件逐步成熟 。短期而言，A股反彈行情仍有機會
延續，主因為美國期中選舉落幕、中國政策環境偏寬鬆，以及A股估值
與情緒指標落在歷史相對低檔 ，具備超跌反彈條件 ，政策面方面 ，人
行可能進一步降息 ，政策利率由 1.25%下調至1%，搭配IPO超募資金
回購、穩定資金加大ETF買入力道，有助於改善市場流動性並提振投資

信心。
• 投資建議： 操作上可優先布局半導體 、AI硬體、通訊設備 、電子零組
件、醫藥及機器人等具成長題材的族群 ，並留意商業航太等新興應用 ，
不過，反彈過程仍須觀察政策落實程度 、企業獲利修復及海外市場變
化，中長期而言，A股仍以科技創新與科技服務實體經濟為核心主軸 ，
相關政策方向並未改變 ，建議維持科技成長為核心配置 ，搭配具景氣
復甦潛力的週期與消費產業，採取分批布局。

中國10月製造業PMI跌回收縮區間，產需動能同步降溫
• 中國10月製造業PMI跌回收縮區間，產需動能同步降溫：中國10月製造業PMI降至49.3%，低於市場預期，也跌破50榮枯線，
顯示製造業景氣再度轉弱 ，主要受到十一長假前需求提前釋放 ，以及前期海外聖誕訂單集中出貨後進入調整期影響 ，使需求與
生產動能同步放緩，新訂單指數和生產指數皆下降，顯示企業生產活動亦有所收斂 ，不過若與去年同期相比 ，新訂單與生產指
數分別增加0.6及0.1個百分點，顯示製造業基本面仍具一定韌性 ，景氣表現優於去年同期 ，此外企業對未來仍維持審慎樂觀態
度，生產經營活動預期指數為53.4%，雖較前月略降，但仍位於擴張區間。
• 展望後市：隨著專項債加速投放，及8,000億元政策性金融工具逐步轉化為實體投資，基建及製造業需求有望持續釋放，帶動裝

備製造、鋼鐵等產業鏈景氣改善，此外11月為企業衝刺全年營運目標的重要時點，訂單接單與生產活動將較10月回溫，產業表
現方面，科技業因受惠於政策扶持及產業升級趨勢，維持強勁動能，消費品製造業則有望受雙十一購物帶動成長。

民主黨主張監管政策 間接支撐黃金價格表現
• 期中選舉為黃金價格帶來短期支撐：民主黨主導國會加深兩黨政治僵局的擔憂，加上可能推動更嚴格的AI產業監管，市場擔
憂對美國經濟成長帶來負面影響，進而增加聯準會採取寬鬆貨幣政策的可能性，同時美元走貶也是上週支撐金價的因素之一。
• 央行需求基本面穩健：世界黃金協會最新報告指出 ，各國央行在9月持續增加黃金持有量，當月淨購買量達61噸，為今年迄
今最高的單月總量。同時數據顯示，全球各國央行所申報的黃金淨購買量為228噸，較去年同期下降 7%。
• 觀點：央行購金需求維持高檔，但相較去年同期累計未進一步放大，同時高水準的美債殖利率排擠黃金的避險需求。在此情
境下，短中期黃金價格能否進一步突破的關鍵，可能取決於聯準會貨幣政策的調整空間。雖然市場大致排除激進升息的預期，

但緩步升息仍是基本情境，預料近期黃金價格將在每盎司4,000~4,200震盪。消息面來看，美伊衝突維持僵局，不過若是荷
姆茲海峽議題能有重大突破，將為黃金帶來進一步上漲空間。
資料來源：世界黃金協會，資料統計至9月底，2026.11.4。

期中選舉牽動，上週國際油價大幅震盪
• 選舉結果令國際油價大跌：選舉結果出爐後，市場預期民主黨將在預算與軍事決策上牽制總統川普，促使中東僵局加速透過
外交協商落幕。結果公布當天布蘭特油價單日大跌約 9%，至每桶94美元，臨近週末則小幅反彈。上週油價整體收跌約16%，
主要反映選舉風險溢價消退，不過美伊僵局未變，油價仍在100美元附近震盪。
• 中東原油出口接近戰前水準，但成品油出口仍大幅落後：波斯灣國家正致力原油出口替代方案，目標擺脫荷姆茲海峽航運依
賴。石油出口量近幾週雖有所回落，但已接近戰前平均水準；然而，成品油出口動能恢復緩慢，目前仍遠不及戰前平均，這
也是造成全球能源通膨的主要原因。
• 觀點：波斯灣國家能源出口替代方案已略見成效，不過由於替代路徑運輸成本較高，能源價格仍居高不下，觀察過去幾週出

口及價格變化，推測在美伊關係未有明確變化的情境下，國際油價將在每桶100美元附近震盪。

付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良

8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。
警 語

2344 華邦電(Winbond)

報告日期： 2026/11/06  研 究 員：王蕎程 (Joe Wang) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要  
 3Q26 稅後 EPS 7.58 元，預估 4Q26 為 8.06 元。 受惠記憶體
報價上漲與產品組合改善，第三季毛利率達 70.57% ，預估第
四季營收 839.78 億元， QoQ +2.0% ，毛利率 70.77% 。 
 擴產與 16 奈米轉進帶動 2027 年位元出貨成長。  高雄廠
Module A 新增產能及台中廠 Flash 擴產陸續貢獻，搭配
DDR4 、LPDDR4 及高容量 NOR Flash 需求，支持營運成長。  
 預估 2026 ／2027 年稅後 EPS 23.29 ／37.45 元。 AI 伺服器與
企業級 SSD 需求為主要動能， CUBE 及矽電容拓展新應用。

一、3Q26 稅後 EPS 7.58 元，預估 4Q26 營收持續季增 
華邦電為利基型記憶體廠，產品涵蓋客製化記憶體、NOR Flash 及
SLC NAND。3Q26 營收 823.31 億元，QoQ +37.6%、YoY +278.2%；
營業毛利 581.01 億元，毛利率 70.57%；營業利益 437.84 億元，營
業利益率 53.18%；稅後純益 354.22 億元， EPS 7.58 元。營收結構
以客製化記憶體占 58%、Flash 占 32%、邏輯 IC 占 9%。其中，客
製化記憶體平均售價季增近 60%，Flash 平均售價季增約 30%，為
第三季成長主因。展望第四季，公司表示 AI 需求維持強勁，部分
PC 與消費性客戶調整拉貨，整體營運仍將成長，惟增幅低於前幾
季。考量合約價格續升及產品組合改善，預估 4Q26 營收 839.78 億
元，QoQ +2.0%；毛利率 70.77%，營業利益 448.28 億元，稅後 EPS 
8.06 元。

二、高雄廠擴產，支應 DDR4 及 LPDDR4 長期需求 
公司持續供應 DDR3、DDR4 及 LPDDR4，受惠主要記憶體原廠調
整產品配置，以及車用、工業客戶產品生命週期長、替換供應來
源不易，長期供貨需求具支撐。高雄廠 Module A 月產能預計由約
1.5 萬片提高至 2.4 萬至 2.5 萬片，新增產能於 2027 年陸續貢獻，
並提高 16 奈米製程比重，同時增加晶圓投入與單片晶圓位元產
出 。 法人看好擴產搭配製程轉進可帶動 2027 年 DRAM 位元出貨顯
著成長，中信預估增幅超過 100%。本報告採新增產能與製程效益
逐步反映的假設，預估 2027 年營收逐季成長。長期方面，董事會
通過 731 億元資本支出，用於高雄廠 Module B 土建、機電及長交
期設備，規劃月產能約 5 萬至 6 萬片，預計 2029 年下半年開始小
量出貨，並以長期供貨協議支應策略客戶需求。 
投資評等：買進

三、AI 帶動 CUBE 與矽電容拓展新應用 
AI 伺服器機櫃內 GPU 、 BMC 、 SmartNIC 及交換器等元件均需 Flash
儲存韌體與設定資料，隨運算及管理架構複雜化，帶動使用顆數
與容量提升。公司預估供應 AI 生態系的 Flash 位元出貨量於 2026
年成長 112%，2027 年再增 67%，其中高容量 NOR Flash 為主要動
能；企業級 SSD 亦增加 DRAM 需求，擴大公司在伺服器供應鏈的
參與 。 法人看好台中廠擴產與高容量產品組合改善 。 新產品方面，
CUBE 主要布局 AI 加速器與推論應用 ， 預計 2027 年下半年增加出
貨；矽電容已小量出貨，既有產能多獲客戶預訂，並透過代工模
式切入 AI 晶片、先進封裝及高速光模組應用，逐步增加產品附加
價值與長期營收來源。  
四. 投資建議：買進，目標價 180 元  
華邦電為全球最大 NOR Flash 廠亦是利基型 DRAM 廠，AI 帶動各
式記憶體需求增加，產能仍供不應求下，獲利將逐季成長。公司
針對邊緣 AI 發展之 CUBE 解決方案將於 2027 年開始貢獻 ， 矽電容
亦為新的業務機會，中長期而言 AI、HPC、IoT、自駕車等新產品
新應用將帶動更多記憶體需求。考量到 1) AI 帶動之記憶體供不應
求將延續至 2028 年、2)華邦電今明年營收獲利創高、3)CUBE、矽
電容具成長潛力，參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 5 倍本益比，目標價 180
元，建議買進。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
華邦電子於 1987 年九月由華新麗華集團轉投資成立，為自有品牌
IC 設計及製造廠商，並為全世界第五大 DRAM 供應商，且為國內
少數擁有晶圓廠之積體電路設計公司，同時也是全球利基型記憶
體的主要供應商。2008 年公司將邏輯 IC 事業分割成立新唐科技，
新唐產品線以消費性電子 IC 、電腦 IC 兩大產品線為主，並同時擁
有一座 6 吋晶圓廠。華邦電在 NOR Flash 為全球最大供應商，產
品上從中低容量市場拓展至中高容量市場。NOR Flash 主要同業為
旺宏、兆易創新、Infineon。

二.2026 年營運概況及展望

1. 2026 下半年記憶體維持上漲格局，預估 2026 年 EPS 23.29 元 
展望 2026 下半年，預估記憶體求維持強勁下，價格持續上漲，唯
因價格基期已高，加上傳統消費性產品追價力道有限，漲幅不若
上半年，不過預估今年隨著價格上漲，加上製程轉換帶動位元出

貨量增加，及新產能貢獻，營收逐季成長，毛利率持續提升，預
估華邦電 2026 年營收 2,644.05 億元(YoY+195.7%) ， 毛利率 67.17% ，
稅後淨利 1,047.92 億元(YoY+2,545.0%)，EPS 23.29 元。

3. 2027 年記憶體更加緊缺，預估 EPS 37.45 元 
展望 2027 年，AI 帶動之記憶體缺貨狀況延續，加上全球新增產能
有限，緊缺情況更加嚴峻，而儘管終端電子產品需求不一，不過
未來 AI 落地後每單位產品之記憶體搭載量持續成長，使得記憶體
位元出貨提升 ，2027 年公司營運仍正向 。DDR4 在全球三大廠逐步
退出下價格自 2Q25 起漲，且因終端電子產品仍有一定需求，價格
將維持高檔，NOR Flash 及 SLC NAND 亦因供給端增產有限，價格
逐步回升，其中 SLC Nand 因大廠退出，華邦將成為全球最大供應
商，而應用於 AI Server 的高容量 NOR Flash 持續供不應求，預期
將推升價格上行至 2027 年。華邦電製程技術方面，20 奈米 DRAM
產品仍為主要出貨主力，應用於 DDR3、DDR4、LPDDR 產品，16
奈米製程已開發完成，2027 年將有一定比例轉換至 16 奈米；NOR 
Flash 營收穩定成長 ， NAND Flash 24 奈米及 32 奈米 SLC Nand 今年
2Q 貢獻顯著提升。 因應記憶體需求強勁， 公司 Flash 與 DRAM 擴
產計畫進行中，華邦電 2025 年資本支出為台幣 55 億元，2026 年
將提升至 395 億元，其中生產設備資本支出約占 95%，大部分用
於高雄產能及製程提升，少部分用於台中廠製程提升，高雄廠產
能將由現在 15k 擴充至年底的 24k，屆時位元出貨量可望翻倍，同
時啟動高雄 Module B 投資計劃，規劃月產能可達 50k~ 60k，將分
階段擴充，預估 2027 年 1 月動工，2029 年 1 月裝機，2029 年底量
產。2027 年預估 DRAM 、NOR Flash 及 SLC NAND 價格持續上漲帶
動營收獲利成長，預估 2027 年營收 3,833.59 億元(YoY+45.0%)，毛
利率 72.29%，稅後淨利 1,685.15 億元(YoY+60.8%)，EPS 37.45 元。

4. CUBE 及矽電容為新成長動能 
AI 時代來臨，華邦電長期專注於利基型 DRAM ，針對 AI 將主攻邊
緣運算市場，邊緣運算之記憶體容量不需像 AI Server 那麼高，為
華邦電可著墨之區塊，因此華邦電提供 CUBE(Customized Ultra 
Bandwidth Element 客製化超高頻寬記憶體元件)解決方案，CUBE
具備超低功耗和超高性能、小尺寸、高成本效益，從而大幅提升
系統能力、回應時間和能源效率，以滿足邊緣運算平臺上快速增
長的 AI 應用需求。CUBE 優點在於 1)SoC 推疊在上面，散熱效果
較好、2) 可在 CUBE Die 上做穿孔(TSV)，降低成本、3) 3D 推疊
適合較小的終端裝置。目前華邦電與多家客戶開發 CUBE 方案，
預估 2026 年底開始貢獻，2027 年放量。另外因應 AI 晶片功耗劇
增、瞬間電流波動，以及先進封裝的空間限制，矽電容具有極低
的等效串聯電感與寄生電感，能在極短時間內補償電流，維持電

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 3Q26 毛利率升至 70.57%，EPS 7.58 元 
華邦電 3Q26 營收 823.31 億元(QoQ+37.6%、YoY+278.2%)，其中記
憶體事業營收 742.84 億元(QoQ+43.7%)。受惠客製化記憶體平均售
價季增近 60%、Flash 平均售價季增約 30%，合併毛利率提升至
70.57% ， 營業利益 437.84 億元 ， 歸屬母公司稅後淨利 354.22 億元，
EPS 7.58 元。展望第四季，記憶體合約價格持續上升，惟部分 PC
與消費性客戶調整拉貨，預估營收 839.78 億元(QoQ+2.0%)，毛利
率 70.77%，EPS 8.06 元。

二. 2026 年記憶體維持上漲格局，預估 EPS 23.29 元 
展望 2026 年下半年，AI 伺服器需求強勁，帶動 DDR4、LPDDR4
及高容量 Flash 價格持續上漲，惟第四季部分 PC 與消費性客戶調
整拉貨，營運成長幅度較第三季收斂。預估華邦電 2026 年營收
2,644.05 億元(YoY+195.7%)，毛利率 67.17%，稅後淨利 1,047.92 億
元(YoY+2,545.0%)，EPS 23.29 元。

三. 2027 年擴產與製程轉進帶動成長，預估 EPS 37.45 元 
展望 2027 年，AI 伺服器與企業級 SSD 需求持續成長，DDR4、
LPDDR4 及高容量 NOR Flash 供需維持緊俏，加上高雄廠新增產能
與 16 奈米製程轉進，帶動位元出貨增加，CUBE 及矽電容亦逐步
貢獻營收。預估 2027 年營收 3,833.59 億元(YoY+45.0%)，毛利率
72.29%，稅後淨利 1,685.15 億元(YoY+60.8%)，EPS 37.45 元。

## Document 121: 261103_daiwa_Realtek

Source PDF: all report pdf/261103_daiwa_Realtek.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following our results memo, we fine-tune our forecasts for 
Realtek. We believe its near-term demand-rebalancing hiccups have come 
to an end, thanks to its pricing power and its ability to pass on supply-chain 
cost inflation. Given our interpretation of management’s tone that 4Q26 is 
likely to be sequentially flat, we expect Realtek’s business to return to its 
seasonal track from 1Q27. We expect it to continue capitalising on its 
structure growth driver of multi-year/multi-G (gigabits per second) 
connectivity bandwidth upgrade cycle, with any AI penetration adding to 
upside. We reaffirm Outperform (2) call with a higher 12M TP of TWD798.

What’s the impact: Forecast revisions. We fine-tune our 2026-28E EPS 
given that fundamentals are broadly intact heading into 2027. Recall 
management’s commentary at its results call (29 October) of expecting 
4Q26 to be a “prudent and steady” quarter. We interpret this as likely a 
sequentially flat revenue, counter-seasonal after its 3Q26 miss on deeper 
demand rebalancing. Without quantifying its 2027 business outlook, 
management expects the year to be one of steady growth. We forecast 
Realtek’s 2027 revenue to grow by 18% YoY, assuming its business returns 
to seasonal track after 2 years of demand rebalancing hiccups caused by 
customers’ unusual restocking and pre-stocking activities.

Back on track after recent hiccups. After 2 years of anti-seasonal 
performance on customer pre-stocking amid uncertainty over Trump’s 
reciprocal tariffs, followed by component price hikes and supply-chain 
uncertainty related to AI cannibalisation, we expect Realtek’s business to 
return to its seasonal track from 1Q27. We believe it should continue to 
capitalise on its secular driver of multi-year/multi-G bandwidth upgrade 
cycle from both wireline and wireless connectivity, including 2.5G/5G/10G 
Ethernet, 10GPON, managed switches and WiFi 7/8. Its penetration into AI, 
from cloud AI companion chips to edge AI to physical AI, should add to 
growth upside, although any meaningful contribution may take time, 
evidenced by its new product offering/development such as SSD, Ethernet 
hub, optical cable, i3C security and SerDes controllers, to name a few. 
According to management, AI will contribute a high single-digit % revenue 
this year, ramping to over 30% contribution by 2030.

What we recommend: Following forecast revisions, we raise our 12M TP 
for Realtek to TWD798 (previous: TWD750), on the same 4-quarter forward 
PER target of 20x. We reaffirm our Outperform (2) rating with key downside 
risk of any component price hikes leading to worse-than-expected demand.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are broadly in line with consensus, 
ranging from -3% to +2%.

3 November 2026

Back on track after rebalancing

➢ Fine-tuning our forecasts on demand rebalancing after pre-stocking 
➢ Likely back on seasonal track after this year’s hiccups 
➢ Reaffirming Outperform (2) call with a higher 12-month TP of TWD798

Company profile 
Realtek focuses on research, design, development and distribution of integrated circuits (ICs) for 
applications across networking & communication (NC), computer & peripheral (CP) and multimedia & 
consumer (MM). The majority of its revenue comes from NC products, including WiFi, Bluetooth, 
Ethernet and switch controllers. It also provides diversified CP products such as audio codec, IP cam, 
and solid-state drive (SSD) controllers as well as MM products like TV SoC and monitor controllers.

### Page 5

5

Realtek Semiconductor (2379 TT): 3 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 29 Jan 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Realtek’s Board of Directors consists of nine members whose expertise spans industry,
academia, finance, accounting, and management, providing the company with a broad and
diverse range of professional perspectives. Three of these directors are independent,
representing 33 percent of the board and exceeding the regulatory minimum requirement of 20
percent for independent directors. This higher proportion of independent directors enhances
oversight, helps safeguard shareholder interests, and supports more objective decision‑making.
However, an important governance risk remains in the area of board diversity. The board
currently includes only one female director out of nine members, which represents 11 percent of
the total. This limited female representation may constrain the breadth of viewpoints and
experiences reflected in board discussions and could be perceived as misaligned with evolving
stakeholder expectations and best practices on gender diversity in corporate governance.
Capital management 1
In the past 8 years, Realtek's dividend payout ratios were above 80%. As a fabless company, it
does not need high capital expenditure for manufacturing sites. Realtek's business has shown
stable earnings growth over its 30-year history. Thus, we see its dividend policy with a high
payout ratio as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 2
Realtek’s related-party transactions are primarily with G.M.I. Technology Inc. and Greatek
Electronics Inc. Realtek pays certain processing costs to related parties. These payments are
made under standard commercial terms and conditions. Overall, we do not observe any
significant impact on Realtek’s business arising from these related-party transactions.
S Supply chain management 2
Realtek has formulated a set of supplier management regulations in accordance with Realtek's
Supplier Sustainability Management Policy. It requires suppliers to obtain ISO9001 quality
management system certification to ensure that all of its suppliers possess a high level of
environment awareness.
E Water & wastewater management 1
As a fabless company without manufacturing plants, Realtek does not produce much wastewater
compared to manufacturing companies, and thus has a relatively limited impact on the
environment. Realtek has implemented a water conservation plan to increase water efficiency,
such as the reuse of rainwater, re-circulating water in its cooling tower and installing water-
saving devices. Its water saving rate (=reduced water consumption/total water withdrawn)
increased gradually.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
The company reports to the Executive Yuan's Environmental Protection Administration waste
and reporting management system every month on the quantity of waste and its method of
disposal. In 2024, it produced 26.10 tonnes of industrial waste, maintaining over a 90% recycling
rate for hazardous industrial waste.
E GHG emissions 1
Realtek has independently conducted GHG emission inventory and management at its Hsinchu
Headquarters since 2019. In 2022, Realtek implemented a comprehensive ISO 14064-1:2018
GHG inventory and passed third-party verification. As of 2024, the company has completed
GHG emission inventory and third-party verification for all sites within the Realtek Group.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

## Document 131: 261104_daiwa_guc

Source PDF: all report pdf/261104_daiwa_guc.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following its results release on 30 October, we raise our 
forecasts and 12M TP for Global Unichip (GUC). The stock more than 
doubled since our last update (Upgrading: unleashed capacity adding to 
growth, 5 August), yet its recent pullback amid increased market volatility 
looks to be unlocking value again for investment, so we reaffirm our Buy (1) 
rating. Fundamentals have unfolded strongly. According to our market 
research, GUC’s key customer in the AI CPU space has pulled forward its 
order trajectory ahead of the previous schedule amid accelerated demand 
for internal and external AI datacentre build-out. This comes on top of its 
initial ramp of another new accelerator project, followed by the next 
generation AI CPU and accelerator ramps for the same customer, with 
further upside from another new AI accelerator customer in the auto space. 
We suggest investors take advantage of the pullback to bargain-hunt.

What’s the impact: Forecast changes. In light of its key AI CPU customer 
accelerating its ordering trajectory, together with multiple AI projects 
ramping in the next 1-2 years to fire on all cylinders for growth, we lift our 
2026-28E EPS by 8-50% for GUC. We forecast triple-digit % YoY EPS 
growth over 2Q26-2Q27, before moderating to 50% or so into 2028. While 
such strong growth should help sustain its hefty valuation, further rerating 
may be unlikely as this would depend on GUC’s ability to win more AI 
projects than we expect, and more importantly, whether it is able to secure 
enough capacity from the supply chain to accommodate any incremental 
business amid the constrained supply environment.

Margin-accretive at operating level. On top of the AI CPU demand uplift, 
we expect GUC to have a total of 6 major AI projects in mass-production for 
2027, including 4 CPU and 2 accelerators, all for US-based hyperscalers or 
system vendors mainly focusing on N3 process nodes, helping broaden 
GUC’s AI project base. Although these projects appear margin-dilutive at 
gross level, they should be accretive at the operating level, thanks to 
GUC’s prudent opex management on enhanced operating leverage.

What we recommend: Given our forecast changes, we lift our 12M TP for 
GUC to TWD7,800 (previous: TWD5,125), based on a higher 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 67x (previous: 63x), justified by its 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 66% from a PEG perspective. Although further rerating remains 
challenging, we are comfortable with our Buy (1) call due to the recent 
share price pullback unlocking value for investment again. Key downside 
risks to our call would be supply constraints and margin execution.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 23-37% below the consensus 
forecasts as we factor in the supply constraints that will moderate growth.

4 November 2026

Value returns when jitters rise

➢ Lifting forecasts on AI strength and a broadened project base 
➢ Margin dilutive at gross level yet accretive at operating level 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) with a higher 12M TP of TWD7,800

Company profile 
Founded in 1998, GUC is an IC design service provider focusing on AI/HPC, networking, consumer and 
industrial applications.

### Page 5

5

Global Unichip (3443 TT): 4 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 5 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
GUC's Board of Directors (BoD) comprises 8 members, including 4 independent directors (50%
of the board), which is significantly higher than the regulatory requirement of at least 20%. The
Board demonstrates diverse expertise across legal, finance, technology, and business
management. The Chairman and the President are different individuals, ensuring separation of
roles and enhancing governance independence.
Capital management 1
Over the past decade, GUC has maintained a consistently high dividend payout ratio, generally
above 60%, and in most years exceeding 80%. In 2025, the company distributed TWD2,144m in
cash dividends, reflecting its policy of sustaining or increasing dividend amounts over time.
Given GUC’s asset-light business model as an IC design service provider, which does not
require significant capital expenditure for manufacturing facilities, this high payout ratio is
appropriate and aligns with its long-term commitment to shareholder returns.
Related party &
transaction 1
GUC's sole foundry supplier is TSMC, who is also GUC's largest shareholder. As an IC design
service company without onsite production, GUC's relationship with the leading foundry of
advanced process nodes provides it a key competitive advantage, in our view.
S Supply chain management 2
GUC is committed to maintaining long-term partnerships with suppliers and establishing a
sustainable supply chain through measures such as promoting its green action policy and
concept to the entire supply chain, where all its suppliers are required to follow its Responsible
Business Alliance (RBA) regulation. It also periodically conducts supplier audits to ensure the
quality of suppliers.
E Water & wastewater management 1
GUC continues its green living project to reduce water consumption. In 2024, the Company
maintained water-saving measures such as rainwater collection systems, automatic sensor
faucets, dual-flush toilets, and regular inspections of water equipment. These efforts aim to
improve water efficiency and promote conservation across operations.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
As an IC design service provider, GUC does not have in-house manufacturing facilities. It
requires its suppliers to use recycled materials for on-site operation and transportation, and
reduce the amount of expendable resources being used. In order to promote a paperless office,
GUC has established a cloud operating system to reduce the use of paper-based documents.
E GHG emissions 1
GUC adopts energy-saving measures to reduce greenhouse gas emissions and has set annual
electricity conservation targets. In 2024, the Company achieved a 1% reduction in electricity use
and increased green electricity to 39% of total consumption. It also continues to develop low-
power and high-efficiency IP to help minimise environmental impact.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

## Document 134: 261104_ms_CCL

Source PDF: all report pdf/261104_ms_CCL.pdf

### Page 1

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
What's new? Earlier today, a fire broke out at Panasonic's (covered by Kazuo
Yoshikawa) CCL factory in Hsinchu, Taiwan. The fire was extinguished before noon
(see Reuters' report), but Panasonic has yet to issue an official statement in
response to the incident.
Our view: Our checks indicate that this plant has monthly capacity of ~200K sheets

and primarily produces M6/7 and some lower-end BT substrate CCLs. Panasonic also
operates CCL sites in Fukushima, Japan; Guangzhou and Suzhou, China; and
Ayutthaya, Thailand, which remains under construction. Most high-end M7/8 CCL is
produced in Fukushima, while the China sites supply much of its mainstream CCL.
Our supply chain checks suggest Panasonic is reallocating capacity toward high-end
CCL for AI applications – particularly a key US hyperscaler’s in-house ASICs, as high-

end CCL supply remains tight. Although the Hsinchu plant is immaterial to group
capacity, the fire could accelerate this shift, potentially driving near-term customer
order overflow to Taiwanese CCL competitors.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
A fire at Panasonic's CCL factory in Hsinchu may push near-term orderflow to
Taiwanese competitors.
Within our coverage, we see EMC as most likely to receive orders, followed by
TUC and Shengyi Tech (EW).
EMC is also a high-end CCL supplier to Panasonic’s key ASIC customer.
Valuations are attractive for EMC and TUC, and we remain OW on both.

the next 12-18 months.

universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Aspeed (5274.TWO): Servers upcycle to continue driving growth; 3Q26 
GM in line; Buy
6 November 2026 | 8:29PM HKT
Oct revenues in line: Oct revenues +24% MoM (vs. 3Q26 revenues +24% QoQ), 
accounting for 31% of our 4Q26E revenue forecast, largely in line. Looking into 
4Q26, we expect revenue to grow 38% QoQ on robust demand, with GM rebounding 
to 71.9% driven by both product mix upgrade and higher price, reflecting the strong 
demand across general servers and AI servers. For 2027, we expect both AI and 
general servers to drive growth, with customer order visibility now extending into 
2Q27, while a rising AST2700 contribution and the price hike should further lift 
blended ASP . 3Q26 in line: GM declined sequentially on continued material cost 
increases, and lands at 69.5%, in line with our estimates. Opex ratio of 17.3% came in 
above GSe/cons of 15.4%/15.2%, leading operating income to +20% QoQ, or 3% / 
1% behind our / Bloomberg consensus estimates, largely in line. We maintain our 
12m TP at NT$22,000 (implied 53x 2027E PE vs. 80% NI YoY of 2027-28E on avg.). 
Maintain Buy.

We expect 4Q26E revenue to grow at 38% QoQ, driven by BMC demand, a rising 
AST2700 mix, easing supply bottlenecks as a new OSAT partner is introduced in 
4Q26, and another round of price adjustments to pass on higher material costs. Our 
forecast implies average monthly revenue of NT$2.3bn in November-December 
(+13% vs. October). On the margin front, we expect higher pricing to more than 
offset rising material costs, driving a sequential GM rebound in 4Q26E. For 
2027-28E, we expect Aspeed to be driven by 1) robust agentic AI demand, which we 
expect to drive continued growth in AI-related general server BMC shipments, 
supported by solid CPU demand; 2) accelerating AI ASIC deployments, with

Earnings revision: We factor in 3Q26 results and cut our 2026E EPS by 3.8% and raise 
our 2027E EPS by 1.4%, while keeping 2028E EPS unchanged, as we factor in 1) higher 
opex in 3Q26, and 2) higher revenue and GM to reflect the rising price on better product 
mix and higher raw material costs. We raise our BMC shipment forecasts to 
33.5mn/51.5mn/65.4mn units in 2026-28E (+77%/+54%/+27% YoY), with the AST2700 
mix rising to 2%/19%/38% and blended ASP growing 19%/31%/8% YoY. Net net, we 
expect Aspeed’s revenue to grow 103%/97%/45% YoY, with OpM reaching 
53.5%/58.5%/61.2% in 2026-28E.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Aspeed (5274.TWO) 
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$22,000 is based on a target P/E of 40x (in line with 
its 10-year historical trading average) applied to our 2028E EPS, and discounted back to 
2027E at 13.6% CoE. Our key assumptions for CoE include: (1) 1.5x beta (sourcing 
Bloomberg), (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate, and (3) a market risk premium at 6.25% (in line 
with GS house view). 
Key downside risks to our view and TP: 1) A softer-than-expected server market 
demand recovery, 2) slower-than-expected BMC penetration within AI servers, 3) 
intensifying market competition.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

Advanced Energy Solution (6781.TW)
Growth Intact Despite A Longer HVDC Transition
CITI'S TAKE 
We walked away from AES’s 3Q briefing more confident in the BBU growth 
outlook. Resilient low-voltage demand and higher-power product 
upgrades should sustain growth through the HVDC transition, with 
400V /800V commercialization targeted for mid/end-2027E respectively. 
Rising technical complexity should favor AES’s engineering expertise and 
established customer relationships. We trim our TP to NT$1,550 (from 
NT$1,680) on 25x 2027E P/E from 27x reflecting HVDC timing uncertainty. 
Reiterate Buy as rising BBU penetration and content per rack underpin 
structural growth.
3Q26: strong BBU demand drives growth and mix upgrades lift GPM y/y — 3Q 
sales of NT$5.2bn rose 27% y/y and 9% q/q, driven by strong BBU demand and were 
9% above our forecast. GPM expanded 1.3ppt y/y to 36.3% on mix upgrades, though 
it fell 2.8ppt q/q. Op profits rose 34% y/y with OPM up 1.4ppt y/y to 24.6%. Net profit 
of NT$1.1bn was up 34% y/y, 3% above our forecast but 5% below consensus. Higher 
opex and lower non-op income partly offset the sales beat.
Low voltage BBUs retain a substantial growth runway — Mgmt expects steady 4Q 
growth and remains positive on 2027E, with no downward revisions to customer 
demand plans. Continued datacenter buildout should sustain low-voltage BBU 
procurement while HVDC architectures undergo qualifications. Demand for 3kW 
products remains broad across conventional servers and lower power datacenter 
workloads. AES is working with several new CSPs and overseas SIs, with new 
customer contributions expected in 2027E. Rising GPU power, greater BBU 
penetration and higher content per MW should support volumes and mix-led ASP 
growth. Cell prices are rising, but AES indicates stable procurement through long-
standing relationships with suppliers and will seek to pass on cost increases. 
HVDC progress: commercialization likely in 2H27E — High voltage BBU 
commercialization has been delayed to 2H27E, reflecting product development and 
system integration challenges. Currently samples have been delivered and large-
scale testing is underway for 400V products, with mass production targeted for 
mid-2027E and 800V products are scheduled for end-2027E. Mgmt. sees high 
voltage BBU ASP to be 5-10x higher than low voltage products. We believe 
increasing system complexity and qualification requirements favor AES’s 
established engineering capabilities and CSP relationships, though shipment 
timing and margin contribution require monitoring.
n
Angela Hsu AC

Non-BBU development: new applications broaden the growth base — Mgmt
expects non-BBU growth to outpace BBU growth in 2027E as new projects scale.
LEV demand should gradually recover following the inventory correction. Telecom
application has entered mass production in 2026, with mgmt. expecting the
business to double in 2027E. AGV, AMR, forklift and robotics projects involve
leading international customers, with AGV /AMR products expected to enter mass

production in 2H27E. These projects should diversify revenue and extend AES’s
battery pack expertise into additional markets.
Earnings revisions and TP change —We lift our earnings forecasts by 6% for
2026E mainly to reflect stronger sales strength and GPM in 2026E while we keep
our 2027 /2028E earnings estimates largely unchanged. We slightly lower our TP to
NT$1,550 based on 25x 2027E P/E vs. 27x prior reflecting HVDC timing uncertainty.

Advanced Energy Solution (6781.TW)
09 November 2026 Citi Research

Advanced Energy Solution
Company description
Established in January 2020 and listed in March 2021, Advanced Energy
Solution Holdings (AES) is a holding company registered in Cayman Island,
supplying battery packs for LEV (light electrical vehicle such as e-Bikes) and
BBU (battery back-up unit for datacenters) applications. Prior to the
establishment and IPO, AES was a BU focusing on non-IT applications within

its parent company, Simplo Technology (6121.TW).
Investment strategy
We rate AES shares as Buy. Being an industry leader in battery pack design
and manufacturing with a proven track record, we expect AES to benefit from
higher BBU content per rack and deep CSP engagement as the industry
transitions from conventional 48V systems to HVDC architecture. BBU now
contributes > 70% of sales and should remain the key earnings driver as AI
rack power continues to rise. We forecast earnings CAGR of 27% in 2025-

2028E, supported by strong BBU demand, mix upgrade toward high-power
BBU products and op leverage.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$1,550 is based on 25x 2027E EPS on par with its
historical average since listing and supported by a 2025-28E earnings CAGR
of 27%. We use 2027E as the year of valuation base as we think 2027E can
better reflect its earnings power after the commercializarion of HVDC
products.
Risks
Key downside risks to our earnings forecasts that could prevent the shares

from reaching our target price include: 1) slower-than-expected end demand
from e-Bikes post the pandemic and volaitlity in cloud capex deployment that
could impact datacenter BBU sales; 2) component supply
constraints/logistics challenges, which could lead to slower-than-expected
shipment and top-line momentum; and 3) peer competition, which could
impact sales growth and margins negatively, in our view.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
3Q26 Beat on Margins Reinforces AI Thermal Ramp; TP Maintained at 
NT$4,350
CITI'S TAKE 
AVC reported net profit of NT$11.6bn, topping Coto/cons estimates by 
2%/2% respectively, driven by margins beat partly offset by a non-ops FX 
loss. GPM improvement was above expectations on a richer liquid cooling 
mix, yield gains and disciplined raw material pass-through. Mgmt. guided 
positively for 4Q26 and 2027, led by AI ASIC ramp-up and Vera Rubin ramp 
up. We model 16% QoQ revenue growth for 4Q26E. GPM guidance also 
points to further sequential improvement on a higher liquid cooling mix and 
scale effects. Mgmt. reiterated 2026E/27E capex of NT$15bn/15-20bn, 
alongside 50%+ cold plate capacity expansion, underscoring capacity 
conviction. We fine-tune our earnings forecasts to reflect updated guidance 
and maintain TP at NT$4,350 (25x 2027E EPS). Reiterate Buy.
3Q26 results beat on margins — AVC’s reported 3Q26 net profit of NT$11.6bn 
(+20% QoQ/+52% YoY) beat Citi/cons forecast by 2%/2%, driven by better-than-
expected margins, partly offset by a non-ops loss. 3Q26 GPM came in at 33.9% 
(+1.3ppts QoQ/+7.7 ppts YoY), above both Citi/cons estimates, mainly due to 
continued mix improvement, a higher liquid cooling contribution, yield gains, and 
disciplined pass-through of raw material price fluctuations to customers. Non-ops 
included a NT$304mn FX loss, which partially offset the strong core business.
ASIC ramp and capacity guidance underpin long-term outlook — Mgmt. guided 
positively for 4Q26 and 2027, led by AI ASIC project ramp-up and early Vera Rubin 
generation contribution. We now model 16% QoQ revenue growth for 4Q26E. GPM 
guidance also points to further sequential improvement vs. 3Q26, driven by a higher 
liquid cooling mix and scale effects. Mgmt. expects server compute tray liquid 
cooling penetration to reach 100% by end-2027. Mgmt. reiterated 2026E/26E capex 
guidance of NT$15bn/15-20bn, alongside 50%+ cold plate capacity expansion, 
reinforcing confidence in capacity build-out.
Implications — We fine-tune our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts to reflect updated 
guidance and outlook. We maintain our TP at NT$4,350, still based on 25x 2027E EPS. 
We believe AVC’s solid supply position across key customers’ AI GPU and ASIC projects, 
combined with a sustained growth trajectory, continues to support its valuation. With 
visibility extending into 2027, we see limited near-term read-through risk from the 
ongoing platform transition. We reiterate our Buy rating on AVC.

5
Bull/Bear: Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)

### Page 6

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
10 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Asia Vital Components
Company description
Asia Vital Components (AVC), founded in 1991, is a global leader in thermal
solutions, originally focusing on PCs but now specializing in servers and
networking. With Furukawa Electric (Japan) as its largest shareholder, AVC
provides a comprehensive range of cooling components, including vapor
chambers (VCs), fans, cold plates, CDUs, and manifolds, as well as chassis

and assembly services for PCs and servers, enabling holistic thermal solution
designs and one-stop shop services. AVC's diverse product portfolio supports
various sectors, including consumer IT, cloud infrastructure, transportation,
energy and networking.
Investment strategy
We rate AVC a Buy. AVC is well-positioned for the liquid-cooling transition,
with leadership in cold plates and broad participation across Nvidia and CSP
projects. Its high-volume machining capability, manufacturing uniformity,

and Vietnam-based capacity provide it with a defensible position as
Blackwell and future deployments expand. The company captures a wider
share of the thermal BOM, across coldplates, manifolds, QDs, and CDU
opportunities, providing a multi-layered earnings engine.
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum
and the expansion of its addressable market. Our TP of NT$4,350 is derived
from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified

given the company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment,
rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing value of thermal
content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural
drivers still in place, we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its
historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported by other qualitative factors such as cold-

plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not
been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price
include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2) heightened
price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling
components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:

rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

### Page 9

10 November 2026 Citi Research

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

 현재주가 (10/29) 108,300원  
 상승여력 19%

 52주 고/저 206,000원 / 105,100원  
 외인지분율 39.05%  
 배당수익률 0.00%  
 주요주주 현대자동차 외 5 인

주가수익률 (%) 1개월 3개월 12개월  
 절대 (6.6) (10.0) (6.5)  
 상대 8.0  (14.2) (35.7)  
 절대 (달러환산) (5.2) (2.7) 0.2

3Q26 영업이익 1.8조, 핵심 하락 원인은 인센티브 
환율효과는 전분기 대비 -5,330억 작용했으나, 기말환율 하락에 따른 판매보증비의 급감 
(6,200억, -9,250억 qoq)으로 상쇄되었다 판단. 인센티브 비용 증가세는 -6,060억원의 영
향을 미쳤으며, 대부분 유럽지역 EV2 판매를 위한 딜러 인센티브에서 발생 추정. 
외생변수는 능력 밖의 일, EV 전략에 대해서는 장기성장에 무게 
중동 분쟁 지속, 환율 하락과 같은 외생변수의 부정적 영향을 제외한다면 단기 영업적 측면
의 실적 악재는 BEV 인센티브 지급기조의 변화가 없을 것이라는 점. 중장기 글로벌 BEV 
시장의 입지 강화를 단기 전사 수익성보다 중시하는 전략을 채택. 
마찬가지로, 4분기가 환율 효과의 본격화 
3Q26과 반대로 qoq 기말환율 대비 낙폭 확대할 평균환율 (QTD 1,338원) 효과로 환관련 
감익 본격화 예상. 즉, 판매보증비의 정상화 (qoq 반등)로 인해 수익성 추가 악화 가능성 존
재. 완성차 판매량의 경우 경쟁사 대비 견조했던 2Q-3Q26의 기조를 유지할 것이라 예상. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 129,000원으로 하향 
목표주가 하향 근거는 산정 기준을 FY27F로 변경함과 동시에 FY27F 자동차사업 NOPAT 
예상치 하향과 현대모비스 지분가치 하락. 밸류에이션상 저점에 도달했다는 의견은 FY27F 
EPS의 yoy 하락으로 설득력 약화. 중장기 BEV 시장 입지 강화에 대한 밸류에이션 프리미
엄 반영은 글로벌 전동화 속도에 달려 있음. 주가 상승 트리거는 1) 외생변수의 개선, 2) 신
차 출시를 통한 상품성 강화, 3) BEV 시장 점유율 확대 이후 점진적 인센티브 축소, 4) 20조
원 수준의 순현금 기반 발생 가능한 사업적 결정.

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 
2026-10-14

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 820,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 366,500 원 
상승여력: 123.7%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 및 환율 영향으로 시장 예상치 하회 예상 
Key: 4Q26부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 계약 수주 모멘텀 기대 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 4 행정 엔진&FDC으로 주가 상승여력 
충분. 매수

3Q26 Preview: 컨센서스 하회하는 실적 기록 예상 
3Q26 연결 매출액 6조 464억원(+36.9% YoY, -4.5% QoQ), 영업이익 9,230억
원(+65.6% YoY, -11.2% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(1조 70억원)를 하회하는 실적 기
록할 것으로 추정한다. 이전 분기 대비 중대재해 및 하계휴가, 그리고 추석 연휴에 따른 
조업일수 감소 효과와 더불어 큰 폭의 환율 하락 영향(약 500억원) 때문이다. 홍범도
함 화재 및 임단협 관련 일회성 비용은 이번 분기가 아닌 4Q 에 반영될 것으로 예상한
다. 해양 부문에서 트리온 FPU 프로젝트 C/O에 따른 일부 환입 발생한 것으로 추정
되며, 해양 도크에서 컨테이너선 선체 블록 일부 건조로 고정비 감소효과 일부 상쇄 중
이다. 다만 올해 연내 혹은 내년 초 추가 해양 수주 없을 시 고정비 상승 우려 확대될 
전망이다. 동사 올해 상선 수주 목표(114.7억불)를 이미 초과하는 수주 실적 기록한 상
황이며, 고마진 선종인 LNGC 및 VLGC/VLAC 중심으로 수주했기 때문에 ‘28~’29
년 구간 경쟁사 대비 뚜렷한 선종 믹스 효과를 기대한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 82 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 97 만원에서 82
만원으로 하향한다. 중국의 공격적인 캐파 증설 지속과 더불어 중국 대형 조선사들의 
글로벌 선주가 발주한 LNGC 물량 및 원가 경쟁력 확대에 따른 상선 부문의 멀티플 
조정이 불가피하다는 판단이다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클
과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지
속되고 있는 중으로 파악된다. 올해 4 분기부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 부문의 본격적
인 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 전망이며, 해당 모멘텀에 따른 주가 상승
여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)를 유지한다.

## Document 366: EcoNews of 2 November 2026 (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/EcoNews of 2 November 2026 (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
5
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.40 / 2 November 2026ECONEWS
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
ADVANCED ECONOMIES
In brief. Data releases confirmed resilient to strong growth and
above-forecast inflation in most advanced economies, with France lag-
ging behind and earning a rating outlook downgrade from Fitch as a
result. Fed, ECB and BoJ remained on hold, as expected, and on track
for December hikes. Oil continues to fluctuate between escalation fears

and hopes for a return to the negotiating table. The tech sell-off in
equity markets intensified despite strong earnings, as markets balk at
large capex plans. This helped bond yields ease from recent highs.
EUROZONE
Resilience confirmed despite tightening credit. GDP expanded by a
stronger-than-expected 0.4% q/q in Q3 (0.2% ex-Ireland), with Spain,
Germany and the Netherlands contributing to the bulk of non-Irish
growth. We increase our growth forecast to 1.6% in 2027 (+0.1pp),

driven by further improving sentiment in manufacturing. Bank lending
conditions tightened further in Q3 for businesses, especially SMEs,
driven by France and Germany, while corporate and housing-loan de -
mand weakened. The ECB kept its deposit rate at 2.5% and signaled it
was firmly on track for a (likely final) December hike. President Lagarde
also confirmed she would step down early, on February 7, setting off
the succession race. EU leaders are expected to decide jointly on the

three Board members departing between January and May (President,
chief economist, and market operations), likely at their mid-December
meeting. Official candidates to succeed Lagarde are BIS Head Pablo
Hernandez de Cos and former Dutch central bank Governor Klaas Knot.
To watch next week: October flash CPI (Wednesday).
France: Another growth setback, with silver linings
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q in Q3 as a sharp drop in aeronauti -
cal production hit exports and investment. However, services out -

put grew by a robust 0.3% q/q, and household consumption also
grew more strongly than forecasted (+0.4%). While 2026 will ine -
vitably be knocked down (to 0.3%), we forecast 0.8% for 2027.
Nevertheless, Fitch issued an unscheduled rating outlook downgrade
to negative territory, on grounds that fiscal, growth and ad -
justment prospects have deteriorated since its August review.
A new review could be concluded as early as February. Harmonised
inflation accelerated to 3.8% y/y in October, with both energy and

non-energy contributing. Meanwhile, producer price inflation accele -
rated to 8.2% y/y in September. Insee’s index of household confidence
fell to 80, matching its 2013 all-time low, driven by a decline in ex -
pectations of its own financial situation. Private payroll employment
declined by 26,000 over Q3 (-0.1%). However, in previous quarters, the
decline in payroll employment was more than offset by the increase in
self-employment (not released until November 27).

To watch next week: September industrial production (Thursday); Sep-
tember foreign trade (Friday).
Germany: Growth holds up as inflation slows
GDP grew by 0.2% q/q in Q3, with consumption rising slightly but in -
vestment declining and export growth slowing. HICP inflation fell from
3.3% to 2.8% in October following energy-tax cuts and fuel discounts,
but core inflation also slowed, to 2.1%. Business confidence (Ifo survey)
reached its highest level in more than three years, led by manufactu -

ring, which is back in positive territory for the first time since 2023.
By contrast, consumer confidence deteriorated to its lowest since May,
due to growing concerns about inflation, which are weighing on consu-
mers’ spending intentions.
To watch next week: September retail sales (Monday); September fac-
tory orders (Thursday); September industrial production (Friday).
Italy: Stagnant growth meets energy-driven inflation surge
GDP was flat in Q3, missing expectations, as positive domestic demand

was offset by net exports. Economic sentiment improved, but HICP in -
flation jumped from 4.2% to 5.6% in October, its highest level since
September 2023, mainly because of regulated energy prices; core in -
flation also rose, to a more moderate 2%. More positively, economic
sentiment rose to 100.2 in October 2026 (from 99.4 in September),
despite the Eurozone decline. The improvement was driven by services
and construction. The Italian cabinet approved a budget focused on

middle-class tax cuts, financed partly by levies on banks, energy and
gambling companies. x
UNITED KINGDOM
A cautious budget buys breathing space. The budget offered 98 small
cost-of-living alleviation measures and £4.1 billion in household ener-
gy cost relief, while only modestly increasing taxes, including a tem -
porary rise in the bank surcharge. Its short-term stance is slightly ex -
pansionary, while major structural decisions on defence, pensions and

welfare have been postponed. The official budget watchdog, OBR, cut
its 2027 growth forecast to 1%, and firms’ inflation expectations conti-
nued to rise. UK gilts outperformed and equities advanced after the
budget. GBP rose to a six-week high against the dollar and a 19-month
high versus the euro as markets price a near-certain Bank of England
rate hike (to 4%) at this week meeting. We agree but, unlike market
pricing, we do not expect it to hike further.

To watch next week: Bank of England interest-rate decision (Thursday).
UNITED STATES
Fed awaiting more information despite strong growth. The Federal Re-
serve held its target range at 3.75–4.0%, with two members voting for a
25 bp increase, as Chair Warsh highlighted stronger growth, favourable
employment conditions and inflation that remains too high. Q3 GDP
accelerated to 3.4% annualised, powered by resilient consumption and
a 12.7% jump in business investment. Despite jobless claims remaining

near multi-decade lows, compensation costs adjusted for inflation de-
clined 0.4%, marking the second consecutive quarter in which labour
cost growth trailed price increases.
To watch next week: Midterm elections (Tuesday); October ISM ma -
nufacturing (Monday) and non-manufacturing (Wednesday); Q3 non-
farm productivity (Thursday); October non-farm payrolls (Friday).
JAPAN
BoJ on hold with underlying inflation “consistent with the 2% tar -
get”. The Bank of Japan kept its policy rate at 1.25%, with one dissent

in favour of a hike. Its outlook judged underlying inflation to be
consistent with the 2% target, a major shift in its assessment, although
Governor Ueda mentioned risks on both sides in his remarks, stres -
sing in particular the risk of inflation overshooting the target. It signals
continued gradual monetary adjustment, with a next hike expected in
December. Alongside the rate hold, the BOJ announced it would near -
ly triple the pace of ETF sales starting in January, to approximately

JPY 860 billion per year. Japanese equities shrugged off the announ -
cement.
To watch next week: Minutes of the September Bank of Japan meeting
(Thursday); September wage growth (Friday).

### Page 2

6
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.40 / 2 November 2026
The bank
for a changing

Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
EMERGING ECONOMIES
ASIA
China: Monetary policy reform and no growth signals
The PBOC disclosed a 1.25% overnight reverse-repo rate and an -
nounced that the overnight rate would become its principal policy
lever, starting 2nd November. It also pledged to develop cross-border
renminbi payments and introduced a liquidity backstop for non-bank

financial institutions. The 5th Plenum of the 20 th Central Committee of
the CCP focused on party discipline and crackdown on corrupt cadres,
without broader policy signals. Chinese equities had their worst month
since 2016, falling 8.5% in October, as doubts over AI earnings deepe -
ned investor pessimism. The PMIs published on 30 th October suggest
economic activity is losing momentum; in the manufacturing sector,
the RatingDog PMI fell slightly to 52 (from 52.1) while the official PMI

fell more sharply and dropped below 50 (49.3 vs. 50.1 in the previous
month).
South Korea and Taiwan: AI boom lifts growth even higher
In Korea, GDP grew by 1.0% q/q and 3.4% y/y in Q3, supported by boo -
ming semiconductor exports linked to global AI demand. The expansion
was uneven, however, as private-consumption growth slowed marke -
dly. In Taiwan, GDP surged by 3.2% q/q and 14.4% y/y in Q3, beating
expectations and accelerating from the previous quarter as technology

firms expanded capacity to meet strong demand.
EMEA
Poland: Lower inflation gives monetary policy some room
Headline inflation declined from 4.0% to 3.5% in October as electricity,
gas and transport fuel prices rose more slowly, helped by a temporary
fuel-price cap. Rates are likely to remain unchanged in the near term,
although renewed zloty weakness means a hike cannot be ruled out.
Saudi Arabia: Reserves fall as regional pressures intensify
Official reserve assets declined by 4.4% m/m in September to

SAR 1.75 tn, their lowest level since December, with foreign-currency
assets recording particularly sharp monthly and annual declines.
Türkiye: Pressure on lira and FX reserves
The lira headed for its largest monthly fall in 15 months and cen -
tral-bank reserves came under renewed pressure. This is the conse -
quence of the warning by MSCI that Türkiye could lose its emer -
ging-market status if measures to improve transparency and market
supervision following the Fund scandal are deemed insufficient.

LATIN AMERICA
Brazil’s post-election rout: stocks and real plunge, yields surge. Bra -
zilian markets, expecting a Flavio Bolsonaro victory, sold off sharply
after Luiz Inacio Lula da Silva won a fourth presidential term. The Ibo-
vespa fell 14% Monday — its largest daily drop since COVID-19 — and
ended the week down 18%. The real fell over 11% intraday and ended
the week down 8% against the euro. Meanwhile the yield on Brazil’s
10-year government bonds rose 250 bp to 15.5%, reaching its highest

level in over a decade. The selloff reflects fiscal concerns over pledges
to raise the minimum wage and Bolsa familia benefits and to offer free
public transport on weekends and bank holidays. Finance Minister Da-
rio Durigan’s pledge to seek a higher 2027 primary surplus, without a
new target, did little to reassure markets.
COMMODITIES
The oil market continues to fluctuate between escalation fears and
hopes for a return to the negotiating table. Brent prices were down by

5% over the week, reaching USD 109/b on Monday. Tensions on physical
supply continue to decline. Ditto for diesel crack spreads. On the back
of geopolitical tensions, lower-than-requested EU stock level and spe-
culation over a cold snap in Europe in November, European gas prices
(TTF) recorded another strong weekly rise and have breached the
EUR 100/MWh for the first time since Dec 2022). The Asian gas bench-
mark (JKM) is following the same trend but at a slower pace (4.6% w/w

to USD 32 /MBtu, equivalent to around EUR 96.3 /MWh).
TO WATCH NEXT WEEK
Global: Manufacturing PMIs for EM and final DM prints (Monday); final
services and composite PMIs (Wednesday).
y/y = year-on-year
m/m = month-on-month
bp = basis point
pp = percentage point
mb = million barrels
MBtu = Million British Thermal Units

## Document 375: Eurozone_ Improving business sentiment, driven by tech, is spreading to other sectors (2026-10-14)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Eurozone_ Improving business sentiment, driven by tech, is spreading to other sectors (2026-10-14).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

14 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
EUROZONE: IMPROVING BUSINESS SENTIMENT, DRIVEN BY TECH, IS SPREADING TO OTHER SECTORS
Stéphane Colliac & Tarik Rharrab
*LATEST OBSERVATION: 5 OCTOBER 2026  **ESTIMATE
The September PMI surveys confirm that growth in the Eurozone is getting stronger, boosted in particular by new export orders, which are
still rising. Sectors that were already performing well – technology and capital goods (including for defence) – are continuing to grow, while

those that had been struggling until now – aeronautics, consumer goods and financial services – are seeing their production indices recover.
This strong performance is particularly noteworthy, given that it has not yet been undermined by higher inflation.
The heatmap shows the PMI index readings: a reading above 50 indicates expansion, while a reading below 50 signals contraction compared to the previous
month. For each indicator, sectors are ranked in descending order based on their September 2026 reading.

Shades of red represent values above 50, while shades of blue represent values below 50: the intensity of the colour increases the further away they are
from this threshold.
Production growth spreads to more sectors
The PMI surveys on business sentiment confirm that business activity in the Eurozone is expanding at a faster pace and spreading to more sectors.
Household-consumption-related sectors (goods and services), which were adversely affected in the spring by the outbreak of the conflict in the Middle

East, do not appear to be negatively impacted by the rising inflation observed since August. The aeronautics sector, where production has mainly been
limited by supply constraints, is also showing signs of improvement.
SOURCE: S&P GLOBAL, BNP PARIBAS

Chart of the Week / 14 October 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
These trends, although still moderate, come alongside a drive to upgrade equipment, fuelled by investment in digital technology, electrification
(which is supporting the automotive sector) and defence. The momentum in technology goods and services further strengthened in September.
The production index for computer and electronics hit a five-year high, while the software services production index stayed close to its July peak.

Financial services are also enjoying a significant improvement, reflecting the healthy lending momentum despite interest rates starting to rise
(see our analysis).
Growth driven by exports
Exports drove growth in the first half of 2026, both across the Eurozone as a whole (see our analysis) and among its key members (notably
Germany). This momentum is also increasing, as indicated by the ‘new export orders’ component of the various PMI indices. As was the case with

production, there has been a particularly noteworthy increase for capital goods (technology and machinery) and related services, particularly digital
services. This trend is playing out more broadly, as, in September, new export orders rose across all sub-sectors, with the exception of intermediate
goods and consumer goods, where contraction recently eased. There has been a significant improvement in the transport sector, particularly in
the aeronautics industry, which has major markets in Asia. Recent data on French exports show that aeronautics exports to this region are rising

once again.
The convergence between rising production and increasing export-order backlogs shows that Eurozone growth is based on an increasingly solid
foundation. Inflationary pressures are not holding it back for the time being.

## Document 390: GLOBAL MARKETS CALL_ Air Coming Out Of AI Bubble

Source PDF: all report pdf/GLOBAL MARKETS CALL_ Air Coming Out Of AI Bubble.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-08
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Air Coming Out Of AI Bubble
Date:    1 hour ago
GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Air Coming Out Of AI Bubble

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Air
Coming Out Of AI Bubble
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 8 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Gaël Gaborel - OrbisTerrae / Unsplash

### Page 2

The AI selloff went global last week. In the US, Democrats swept
Congress in the midterm elections on Tuesday, November 3, surfing on a
huge wave of voter hostility toward new data centers. On Thursday,
investors dumped the AI trade worldwide, and Asia’s chipmakers took the
brunt of the wipeout. Overseas earnings are still climbing, but valuations
keep falling. We see the AI backlash as a speed bump, not a roadblock, at
home and abroad. Here’s more:

I. Stock Market Performance
Global sector leadership flipped this week according to the FTSE indexes
(chart). Defensive sectors held up best, with Real Estate up 2.2% and
Consumer Staples up 0.7%, while Consumer Discretionary led with a
2.7% gain. Technology (-6.7%) and Telecommunications (-7.4%) were
near the bottom. Energy was the worst performer, down 10.4%, as fears
of a post-midterm re-escalation in the Middle East faded. The FTSE World
index fell 2.5%.

The US has been gaining ground since we lowered our "Go Global"
investment strategy to market weight on October 18. The US MSCI’s
share of the All Country World MSCI’s market cap has climbed to 65.3%,
up from 62.4% in March (chart). The gains have come at the expense of
everyone else. The Emerging Markets MSCI’s share has dropped to
11.1% from 12.1% in mid-October.

### Page 3

The AI-heavy markets were at the bottom of the country ETF rankings this
week (chart). Taiwan fell 11.7% in dollars, South Korea 10.4%, and Japan
5.0%. This year’s laggards caught bids. South Africa led with an 8.5%
gain, followed by India at 6.6%. The US ETF fell 2.7%, slightly worse than
the All Country World ex-US ETF’s 1.8% decline.
Both the US and overseas ETFs have pulled back from their recent highs
(chart). Both remain in solid uptrends that began in late 2022.

### Page 4

II. Stock Market Earnings & Valuation
The All Country World ex-US MSCI’s forward earnings rose to yet another
record high this week (chart). It has soared since 2025 after going
nowhere for most of the previous decade.
Forward earnings is up 41.0% y/y, while forward revenues is up 11.2%
(chart). During solid economic expansions, forward revenue growth tends
to hover around 5%. Today’s double-digit pace confirms an economic
boom underpinning the global bull market.

### Page 5

Valuations continue to fall even as earnings soar (chart). The US MSCI’s
forward P/E is down to 18.9. Japan’s has dropped to 14.2 from 14.7 a
week ago. The All Country World ex-US trades at 12.0. The Emerging
Markets MSCI has slipped to single digits and is at 8.6 now.
III. Bonds
Sovereign bonds continued to rally worldwide this week as investors fled
the AI trade (chart). The US 10-year Treasury yield fell to 4.86%. The UK's
yield remains the highest in the group, at 5.16%, followed by Australia’s

5.00%. France’s yield has dropped to 4.77%, back below the US yield.
Japan’s yield is down to 2.63%.

### Page 6

IV. Focus on South Korea & Taiwan
South Korea's forward earnings is still rising to record highs, though more
slowly than earlier in the year (chart). The stock market isn’t buying it. The
MSCI South Korea stock price index is down more than 45% from its
record high. Its forward P/E has collapsed to 3.4, the lowest in over 20
years. Investors are betting that analysts will have to slash their earnings
estimates.
Taiwan tells the same story, but with a more sensible valuation. Its forward

earnings is also at a record high. Its forward P/E has dropped to 14.4 from
23.2 earlier this year (chart).

### Page 7

Fundamental data suggest South Korea and Taiwan should continue to
benefit from US demand for technology hardware. US imports of selected
technologies rose to a record $61.3 billion in September (chart).
Asia supplies much of that hardware. US imports from Vietnam rose to
$28.7 billion in September, and imports from Taiwan rose to $28.4 billion
(chart). Imports from South Korea rose to $19.2 billion. A slower US data
center buildout would delay that demand, not derail it. We would view a

slower pace as more sustainable over a longer period of time.

### Page 8

clause.
Yardeni QuickTakes © 2026 – Unsubscribe

## Document 395: Hotter US inflation strengthens the case for a December Fed hike

Source PDF: all report pdf/Hotter US inflation strengthens the case for a December Fed hike.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

US consumer price inﬂation came in hotter than
expected, with headline CPI rising 0.4% month-on-month and
core CPI up 0.3%, both a tenth of a percentage point above
expectations. No year-on-year rates were published because
the government shutdown meant no price data was collected
in October 2025
James Knightley
Used vehicle prices fell sharply in October
There was some softness in the details of today’s US CPI report,
with new vehicle prices rising just 0.2% month-on-month and used

vehicle prices falling 1.2%. But other components were hotter:
medical care commodities rose 0.5%, transportation services
jumped 0.8%, while shelter, the largest component of the CPI
basket, increased 0.3%. Overall, headline CPI rose 0.4% and core
0.3%, both 0.1ppt above consensus.
Following on from Fed Governor Chris Waller's comments earlier
today that he backs a 25bp hike in December and could even opt
for a 50bp hike, Treasury yields have pushed higher, with the 10Y

and 2Y up 6bp and 13bp, respectively. Markets are now fully pricing

Nonetheless, hopes of a Middle East de-escalation are pulling

for the ﬁrst time since mid-July. If that continues, it should provide
a meaningful drag on November headline inﬂation, but we won't
get that data until the day after the December FOMC meeting.
The Fed may get a sneak peek of that data, giving it the conﬁdence
to only raise rates by 25bp. We are not forecasting any further hikes

in 2027 as interest rate-sensitive sectors cool further, with housing
costs and lower energy prices amid weak wage growth dampening
price pressures in the economy.

► 近一週美股 、日股及新興市場表現遜於港股
及歐股 ，因市場擔憂民主黨全面掌控國會恐
影響AI基礎建設擴張進程 ，令股市承壓 ，即
便總統川普表態接受共和黨敗選結果並願尋
求民 主 黨 的跨 黨 合 作，仍 無 法緩 解 市 場擔
憂，美股主要指數普遍收跌；歐股方面 ，面
對全球科技股評價修正 ，部分資金轉入歐洲

市場尋求避險 ，使歐股近期呈現跌深反彈；
亞股方面 ，日股因整體 AI供應鏈與美國高度
連結而同步下跌 ，港股則是受惠騰訊發布新
AI模型帶動相關版塊大漲。
► 市場擔憂美股科技巨頭財報不如預期 、AI基
礎建設受限及資本開支見頂 、負自由現金流
量等因素 ，導致市場資金從半導體 、資訊科
技與 通 訊 服務 股 大 幅流 出，並轉 進 必 需消
費、醫療保健、公用事業等防禦性產業。

► 美國期中選舉結果揭曉 ，民主黨掌握參 、眾
兩院多數席次 ，將壓縮財政擴張幅度 ，政策

熱門議題
► 黃金價格歷經第二季修整 ，近日呈現大幅反
彈走勢 ，主要係因美國期中選舉後 ，市場憂
心AI產業監管將趨嚴 ，加上晶片與科技股遭
遇大幅修正 ，促使部分資金轉往黃金等防禦
型資產尋求避險。
► 根據世界黃金協會最新報告 ，第3季全球央行
淨買進321公噸黃金，較第二季明顯回升，年
增率達42%，創下自2024年第四季以來的單
季新高，前3季累計買進金額更是高達 675公
噸，顯示黃金儲備已經列入全球央行必需資
產配置而且比重維持穩定增長態勢。
► 長期以來黃金具有抗通膨特性 ，觀察黃金與
聯邦基金利率走勢 ，從2020年之後 ，即便全
球歷經2次升息循環，黃金價格仍呈現穩健上
揚走勢 ，反應其抗通膨保值特性 。此外，美
國財政赤字壓力難消 ，恐令美元長期購買力
被稀釋 ， 加 速 全 球 央 行 去 美 元 化， 有 鑑 於
此，建議黃金納入資產配置。

資料來源：Bloomberg，Factset，凱基證券整理；註：右上產業回報相關性為各產業過去3年之每週報酬相關性
► 醫療類股於美國第三季財報展現強勁的獲利
韌性，實際獲利優於預期程度為主要產業中
表現最佳，背後主要受惠大型製藥企業 (如：
禮來、再生元製藥、吉利德科學)在減重藥、
癌症治療及創新藥領域之需求持續增長 ，帶
動整體產業獲利表現優於市場預期。
► 期中選舉後醫療政策輪廓逐漸明朗 。民主黨
主張支持擴大醫療保險覆蓋 、延續平價醫療
法案補貼以及緩和醫療補助計劃 (Medicaid)
削減，有助提升醫療服務使用率；雖藥價談
判政策恐帶來部分定價壓力 ，但整體政策主
張仍對創新藥 、生技研發及醫療保險產業具
潛在政策支持效果。
► 綜上，在AI投資回報率面臨市場檢驗之際 ，
市場資金持續尋求防禦性配置 。醫療保健類
股與資訊科技類股相關性偏低 ，有助分散投
資組合對單一 AI題材依賴 ，加上第三季獲利
動能轉強 、創新藥需求穩健及產業能見度提
升，後續有望持續受惠資金輪動效應。

財報與政策題材雙利多，醫療類股成市場新寵兒

政策議題 民主黨傾向 共和黨傾向 兩黨共識對製藥產業潛在影響
降低藥價 依《通膨削減法案》框架，擴大醫療 補助計劃(Medicaid)藥價談判適用範圍 傾向透過最惠國定價機制來降低藥 價 兩黨均主張降低藥價 製藥企業面臨定價壓力
美國本土製造 透過研發補助、稅收優惠及產業政策 鼓勵藥廠回流美國生產 透過關稅、供應鏈重組及企業承諾 推動製藥產能回流 兩黨均支持醫藥供應鏈自主化 有利本土藥廠及原料藥供應鏈投資
醫療保險覆蓋
支持延長《平價醫療法案》補貼、擴
大醫療補助計劃(Medicaid)覆蓋範圍，
並緩和該補助計劃的削減
傾向由州政府及市場機制主導醫療
體系，限制聯邦政府支出
醫療的可負擔性為兩黨政策共同焦點，有助
增加就醫人口及處方藥使用量，支撐製藥、
醫療服務及保險業長期需求
FDA審查資源 支持增加FDA審查預算與人力配置 著重監管效率優化，額外資源投入 相對有限 兩黨均支持提升新藥審查效率 利多創新藥與生技公司

### Page 7
